Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on fossils structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 970. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 352 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 970 + O(1/n^352). Summing over all modes and applying the fossils convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
